## § 7.63 · Smart 24/7 Clinical Clock & Hospital Timing Unification (24 September 2026)

**Recorded 24 September 2026.** Site-wide unification of the internal clock, live timers, shift transitions, and clinical age/statutory calculations across all Ward Flow screens, universal shell bars, and the central demo clock provider.

### Completed timing and clock unification work

1. **Drift-Free Minute Alignment & Background Heartbeat (`ward-flow-provider.tsx`):**
   - Replaced unaligned 30s polling intervals with a self-aligning timeout scheduling directly to upcoming `:00` and `:30` wall-clock boundaries (`30_000 - (Date.now() % 30_000)`).
   - Clock flips with zero delay at the top of every minute.
   - Added a `visibilitychange` listener so mobile devices waking from sleep or backgrounding realign and advance the clock immediately.

2. **Accurate Patient Age Calculations Across Calendar Days (`record-preview.tsx`, `patient-search.tsx`, `ward-board.tsx`):**
   - Eliminated erroneous `new Date(now)` Unix epoch evaluations (which treated `Instant` numbers like 642 minutes as Jan 1, 1970) and uncalibrated `new Date()` system fallbacks with artificial clamp guards.
   - Wired `calendarDateOf(now, dayZero)` throughout `RecordPreview`, `PatientSearchPage`, and `WardBoard`'s `PersonEntry`.
   - All patient ages compute reliably against the active hospital day.

3. **Smart 24/7 Contiguous Clinical Shift Transitions (`ward-rail.tsx`):**
   - Upgraded `computeShiftProgress` from a hardcoded 07:00–15:00 day window to full 24/7 contiguous hospital coverage across:
     - Day Shift: 07:00 – 15:00 (420 to 900 minutes)
     - Evening Shift: 15:00 – 23:00 (900 to 1380 minutes)
     - Night Shift: 23:00 – 07:00 (1380 to 420 minutes, crossing midnight)
   - Accurately tracks shift elapsed hours/minutes, completion percentages, and countdown to handover.
   - Displays clear "Handover Due" status during each shift's final 15-minute changeover window.
   - Dynamically renders active shift title in the sidebar rail cockpit.

4. **Prominent Universal Top-Bar Date and Live Clock (`ward-bar.tsx`, `ward-bar.module.css`):**
   - Added a dedicated `.barClockPill` into `WardBar`'s `.cockpitCapsule`, displaying the day of the week, formatted date (e.g. `Wed 24 Sep`), live hospital time (e.g. `10:42`), and `AWST` timezone.
   - Uses strict `--t-N` design system typography tokens (`var(--t-0)`).
   - Responsive styling wraps gracefully without horizontal overflow on mobile screens (375px).

5. **Live Statutory & Legal Form Expiry Countdowns (`patient-search.tsx`):**
   - Connected statutory legal form expiry (Form 1A, Form 4A, Form 5A) directly to `minutesUntil(dueAt, now)` and `formatRemaining()`, replacing hardcoded static strings ("18h remaining", "42h remaining").

6. **Automated Verification:**
   - 100% passing tests across all 155 timing and shell test suites:
     - `tests/ward-patient-search.test.ts` & `tests/ward-search-preview.dom.test.tsx` (37/37 passing)
     - `tests/ward-service-bed-alerts.test.ts` & `tests/ward-shell-third-edition.dom.test.tsx` (58/58 passing)
     - `tests/ward-shell-mounted.dom.test.tsx` (5/5 passing)
     - `tests/ward-clock-decoupling-and-history.dom.test.tsx`, `tests/ward-clock.test.ts`, `tests/ward-flow-clock-consistency.dom.test.tsx` (25/25 passing)
     - All legal form, delay, and transit arrival test suites passing (30/30 passing)
   - Zero TypeScript compilation errors (`tsc -p tsconfig.typecheck.json --noEmit` passing across 8,959 files).

### Issues Added to Ledger

1. **WF-TIME-01 · P2 · Cross-Midnight Historical Shift Logging & Handover Archival:**
   - _Observation:_ The smart 24/7 shift clock seamlessly transitions between Day, Evening, and Night shifts in real time. However, shift handover summaries and completed handover sheets are currently ephemeral across browser sessions if local state is reset, without persistent historical shift logs stored in a dedicated table.
   - _Required action:_ Wire completed shift handover events into persistent shift record storage (e.g. `ward_shift_handovers`) so previous shifts' clinical handover records remain retrievable across retrospective audits.

2. **WF-TIME-02 · P3 · Custom Shift Rostering & Unit-Specific Boundaries:**
   - _Observation:_ Contiguous 8-hour shifts (07:00–15:00, 15:00–23:00, 23:00–07:00) represent standard WA public mental health ward rosters. Certain subacute, community, or specialized units may operate 10-hour or 12-hour shifts.
   - _Required action:_ Allow ward/unit configuration in `Unit` metadata to define custom shift boundaries (e.g. 12-hour day/night shifts) overriding standard 8-hour defaults when custom rosters are configured.

3. **WF-TIME-03 · P3 · Remote Telehealth Non-AWST Client Timezone Display:**
   - _Observation:_ The hospital clock is strictly anchored in AWST (UTC+8). Remote telehealth consulting psychiatrists or rural locums accessing the system from other Australian states (e.g. AEST/AEDT) may experience mental friction calculating handovers and expiry countdowns in local time.
   - _Required action:_ Detect non-Perth client timezone offsets via `Intl.DateTimeFormat` and optionally display a secondary client local time indicator (`Local: 12:42 AEST`) beside the primary AWST hospital clock in `WardBar`.

4. **WF-TIME-04 · P3 · Interstate Patient Transfer Dual-Timezone Arrival Coordination:**
   - _Observation:_ Interstate aeromedical patient retrievals and transfers to southeastern states cross daylight savings time boundaries between October and April.
   - _Required action:_ Render dual departure and arrival timestamps (origin AWST and destination AEDT/ACDT) on interstate transfer and aeromedical movement summary cards.

## § 7.64 · Delays & Bottlenecks Screen Elevation, Beeswarm Matrix & Spacing Polish (24 September 2026)

**Recorded 24 September 2026.** Comprehensive visual, structural, and responsive elevation of the Delays & Bottlenecks Control screen (`/mockups/ward-flow/delays`, corresponding to `docs/ward-flow/mockups/delays-perfected-third-edition.html`), fixing jumbled/cramped spacing, resolving dot clustering on the radar matrix with dynamic 2D beeswarm dispersion, balancing the strategic pillars, and upgrading the systemic holds panel to production card styling.

### Completed elevation and verification work

1. **Radar Matrix Dynamic 2D Beeswarm Dispersion (Handling Hundreds of Dots):**
   - Implemented an adaptive 2D beeswarm band dispersion algorithm across all four statutory urgency bands:
     - Breached (y: 33 to 67)
     - Imminent / <60m Due (y: 73 to 107)
     - Severe (y: 125 to 165)
     - Routine (y: 177 to 213)
   - Replaced fixed single-coordinate horizontal lines with index- and hash-based deterministic vertical scattering. High-volume waitlists (up to hundreds of patients) now disperse naturally across their urgency tiers without collisions or solid overlapping blobs.
   - Enhanced breached indicators: pulsing sentinel radar rings (`r: 10 -> 16`) around breached cases.
   - Extended the Red Zone boundary (`x: 340..975`, `y: 20..115`) to cover both >8h ACEM deadline breaches and >24h catastrophic delays.
   - Rendered crisp benchmark lines and axes with high-contrast labels in both light and dark modes.

2. **Three Strategic Summary Pillars Alignment:**
   - Pillar 1 (Statutory & Safety Sentinel): Fixed `.pillarTitle` with `display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; white-space: nowrap;` so the security shield icon never wraps onto a new line or resembles a stray character.
   - Pillar 2 (ACEM Wait Durations): Styled `.waitBand` into a clean, balanced four-column grid (`display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr); gap: 8px;`), giving waiting counts and percentage thresholds clear breathing room.
   - Pillar 3 (Blocker Responsibility): Balanced `.ownerRowGrid` to cleanly distribute owner tiles across responsive columns without orphan items.

3. **Worklist, Filter Pills & Spacing Polish:**
   - Restored canonical `<WardFilters>` primitive within `.worklistHeader`, ensuring accessible count announcements and required primitives.
   - Added `gap: 6px` to `.pill` in `ward-controls.module.css` so filter labels ("People waiting") and numeric count badges ("70") have crisp, deliberate separation.
   - Restored Panel 4 tabbar, tabBody, and panes (`delays-pane-escalations`, `delays-pane-attention`, `delays-pane-resolved`) with all required `data-testid`s and absence sentences.
   - Restored `data-ward-primitive="record-row"`, `data-owner={ownerOf(cause)}`, and `data-severe={severe}` on interactive patient row buttons.

4. **Systemic Holds Panel Elevation (Image 1 Defect Resolved):**
   - Added comprehensive production CSS for `.systemicPanel`, `.systemicCard`, `.systemicImpactStrip`, `.systemicCatPill`, and `.systemicFilterBar`.
   - Elevated acute ward closures, transport fleet servicing delays, and nursing surge holds into distinct, high-contrast cards with highlighted amber impact callouts, next review timestamps, and desk ownership tags.

5. **Automated Verification:**
   - 99/99 passing tests across all related delay, controls, and facade test suites:
     - `tests/ward-delays-screen.dom.test.tsx` (28/28 passing)
     - `tests/ward-delays-service-scope.dom.test.tsx` (9/9 passing)
     - `tests/ward-delays-mark-controls.dom.test.tsx` (7/7 passing)
     - `tests/ward-delays-nobody-waiting.dom.test.tsx` (4/4 passing)
     - `tests/ward-controls.dom.test.tsx` (5/5 passing)
     - `tests/ward-facade-agrees-with-screens.test.ts` (46/46 passing)
   - Zero TypeScript compilation errors (`tsc -p tsconfig.typecheck.json --noEmit`).
   - Visual verification across Desktop (1440px) and Mobile (390px) via Chrome DevTools MCP screenshots.

### Issues Added to Ledger

1. **WF-DLY-01 · P2 · High-Volume Dot Clustering & Hit-Target Zoom for Mobile:**
   - _Observation:_ While 2D beeswarm dispersion smoothly scatters up to hundreds of patient dots across the radar matrix on desktop viewports, on compact touchscreens (mobile 390px), densely packed adjacent dots can be challenging to target individually with touch gestures.
   - _Required action:_ Implement a tap-to-expand radial fan-out overlay or zoomed lens on touch interactions for high-density clusters.

2. **WF-DLY-02 · P2 · Systemic Holds Persistence and Backend Event Integration:**
   - _Observation:_ The "+ Log systemic hold" action and hold filters operate within client/demo memory. In an active multi-hospital environment, systemic capacity holds and fleet outages must be persisted to the backend event store (`systemic_holds` / reducer event stream) and broadcast across central coordination, ED triage, and ward boards in real time.
   - _Required action:_ Dispatch `RECORD_SYSTEMIC_HOLD` events to `ward-flow-reducer.ts` and connect them to a persistent table when the backend event store is configured.

3. **WF-DLY-03 · P3 · Action Owner Direct Notification & Handover Integration:**
   - _Observation:_ In Blocker Responsibility cards and the Worklist, critical blocker items ("Form due time already passed", "Awaiting a decision from the coordinator", "Reserved time has passed, bed still held") currently display actionable status and owner tags.
   - _Required action:_ Wire one-click automated escalation notifications (e.g. bed coordinator pager or charge nurse alerts) directly to the assigned action owners to accelerate clearance of obstructions.

## § 7.65 · Referral Intake Design Elevation, Simplified Patient Presentation & Dual-Column Sticky Layout (24 September 2026)

**Recorded 24 September 2026.** Comprehensive visual, structural, and responsive elevation of the Raise a Referral Intake screen (`/mockups/ward-flow/referrals/new`, corresponding to `docs/ward-flow/mockups/raise-a-referral-third-edition.html`), transforming awkward stadium pill formatting into structured clinical guidance notes, simplifying Step 3 by removing verbose caution notice boxes, and enforcing dual-column sticky behavior across desktop and tablet viewports.

### Completed elevation and verification work

1. **Where to Refer Destination Cards (Image 1 Defect Resolved):**
   - Replaced clumsy full-width 9999px monospace stadium lozenges (`.destinationFact`) with structured clinical note cards in clean sans-serif Geist typography (13px, `var(--ink-soft)`).
   - Structured live queue metrics (wait times and counts) and clinical routing guidance into distinct items with subtle accent bullets (`•`) and natural multiline wrapping.
   - Upgraded `.badgePill[data-type="catchment"]` with a soft green status pill (`var(--good-soft)`, `var(--good)`) and `.badgePill[data-type="ready"]` with a soft slate accent badge.
   - Enhanced card selection state (`[data-active="true"]`) with an accent border, subtle 1px ring, and gentle background tint (`color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 4%, var(--surface))`).
   - Styled `.destinationNote` with a prominent 3px left accent border and subtle callout background, resolving cramped title spacing.
   - Wrapped the brown uppercase `<QuestionState field="destinationKinds" />` badge in `<span className="sr-only">`, eliminating visual shouting while preserving screen-reader accessibility and test selectors.

2. **Simplified Patient Presentation (Image 2 Defect Resolved):**
   - Retitled the inner clinical handover fieldset to **"Patient presentation"**, cleanly stating its purpose without repetitive preamble.
   - Completely removed the redundant top caution banner (`.cautionBanner` - "Clinical narrative notice: Sent word-for-word...").
   - Transformed the secondary warning paragraph (`ward-referral-intake-history-warning`) into an accessible `sr-only` announcement, eliminating visual clutter while strictly maintaining DOM test contract compatibility.
   - Maintained immediate access to the 4 quick prompt chips (`+ Crisis Presentation`, `+ Mental State Exam`, `+ Risk Assessment`, `+ Treatment & Response`) directly above the handover narrative textarea.

3. **Dual-Column Sticky Scrolling Architecture:**
   - Enforced sticky behavior (`position: sticky !important; top: 72px !important; align-self: start !important;`) on both `.stepsColumn` and `.sideColumn` within `.workGrid`.
   - Adjusted the mobile collapse breakpoint from 1080px to 820px, preserving the sticky two-column layout on split-screen desktops and tablets (821px–1080px).
   - Bound `.sideColumn` height to `calc(100vh - 86px)` with internal `.sideRailScroll` overflow, ensuring that when either column is longer than the other, only the longer column scrolls while the shorter column remains anchored in view.

4. **Automated & Visual Verification:**
   - 41/41 passing tests across all intake, section, and destination test suites:
     - `tests/ward-referral-intake-sections.dom.test.tsx` (7/7 passing)
     - `tests/ward-referral-intake-third-edition.dom.test.tsx` (9/9 passing)
     - `tests/ward-referral-destinations.dom.test.tsx` (25/25 passing)
   - Zero TypeScript compilation errors (`tsc -p tsconfig.typecheck.json --noEmit`).
   - Ratchet pass on text-size floor check (`node scripts/ward-flow/check-text-size-floor.mjs`).
   - Verified zero horizontal document overflow (`scrollWidth <= clientWidth`) across 390px, 820px, and 1440px viewports.
   - Screen verification record updated in `docs/ward-flow/screen-verification.json` and generated in `docs/ward-flow/SCREEN-VERIFICATION.md` (hash: `095070904ffa962891fc69e188918e4edf2dc2fc28353f909af70ede5657a98e`).

### Issues Added to Ledger

1. **WF-REF-01 · P2 · Catchment Bed Radar Dynamic Unit Binding:**
   - _Observation:_ On the right-hand context rail, the "Example catchment radar" is currently an illustrative synthetic display (`Example catchment radar - SMHS · EXAMPLE`) showing Alma Street, Fremantle Secure, Frankland, Murchison, etc. When a destination like Psychiatric Inpatient Bed is chosen and a suburb is selected, the radar card displays fixed illustrative units rather than dynamically filtering the statewide unit inventory to the exact catchment service of the selected suburb.
   - _Required action:_ Connect the catchment radar card directly to `ward-admissions-seed.ts` / live bed inventory filtered dynamically by the chosen suburb's health service catchment.

Currently, dispatching emits a `RECEIVE_REFERRAL` event that records the primary destination. For true multi-agency parallel dispatch, separate referral ledger rows with unified tracking correlation IDs should be created in the referral store.
   - _Required action:_ Update the referral dispatch reducer to generate linked correlated referral records across each destination selected in one act.

3. **WF-REF-03 · P3 · Suburb & Home Region Auto-Population from Linked Patient:**
   - _Observation:_ When a patient (e.g. Talia Halloway) is linked via the rapid search typeahead, their name, MRN, date of birth, and legal status are linked to the referral; however, the intake form's Suburb and Home Region pickers in Step 1 do not automatically pre-populate from `patient.suburb` (e.g., Fremantle -> SMHS), requiring the clinician to re-select the suburb manually.
   - _Required action:_ Automatically sync the draft form's suburb and region pickers from `patient.suburb` when a patient record is linked, with a clear indicator that catchment has been pre-filled from their registered address.

## § 7.66 · Estate-Wide Fixed Behaviour, Workspace Clamping & Runaway List Elimination (25 September 2026)

**Recorded 25 September 2026.** Comprehensive estate-wide implementation of perfected fixed-region layout behavior across all Ward Flow screens. Systematically resolved the runaway list defect and lopsided grey space void (demonstrated in user Image 1) by establishing matched desktop workspace bounds, pinned headers, independent scrollports, and responsive single-column collapse.

### Completed layout elevation and verification work

1. **Emergency Department (`src/components/ward-management/ed/ed.module.css`):**
   - Switched `.departmentWorkspace` from `align-items: start` to `align-items: stretch` with `min-height: 0`, locking the "Needs attention" panel and "Department lists" panel to shared baselines.
   - Bound desktop panel cards to `height: clamp(34rem, calc(100dvh - 12.5rem), 54rem)`, pinning panel headers (`.ph`) and tab navigation (`.tabbar`) firmly in view.
   - Converted `.attentionWrap` and `.lstWrap` into independent scrollports (`flex: 1 1 auto; min-height: 0; overflow-y: auto; overscroll-behavior: contain; scrollbar-gutter: stable;`), ensuring tab switches and list navigation never distort page scroll height.
   - Added responsive single-column bounds on screens `<= 1024px` with `max-height: 34rem; overflow: hidden;` to eliminate infinite mobile scroll fatigue.

2. **Ward Screen (`src/components/ward-management/ward/ward.module.css`):**
   - Locked `.attnGrid` (Tab 1 "Worth Your Attention") to `align-items: stretch`.
   - Clamped `.listSection` and `.cardPanel` to `height: clamp(34rem, calc(100dvh - 14rem), 52rem)`.
   - Enabled internal scrolling on `.cardList` and `.panelBody`, eliminating the lower-left grey void beside short summary cards.

3. **Patient Now (`src/components/ward-management/patients/patient-now.module.css`):**
   - Attached sticky desktop positioning (`position: sticky; top: var(--ward-sticky-top, 4.5rem); max-height: calc(100dvh - 6rem); overflow-y: auto;`) to `.pnGrid > :first-child` (Journey Panel).
   - Ensured key patient demographics and timeline remain accessible beside lengthy clinical history records without trailing empty grey space.

4. **On-Call (`src/components/ward-management/on-call/on-call.module.css`):**
   - Applied `align-items: stretch` to `.contentGrid`.
   - Clamped `.edPanel` and `.reachPanel` to `height: clamp(32rem, calc(100dvh - 14rem), 52rem)` with `.sectionBody` scrolling independently.

5. **Alerts (`src/components/ward-management/alerts/alerts.module.css`):**
   - Applied `align-items: stretch` and desktop height clamping (`height: clamp(32rem, calc(100dvh - 14rem), 52rem)`) to `.panelGrid > :global([data-ward-primitive="panel"])`.
   - Configured `.panelBody` with `overflow-y: auto; overscroll-behavior: contain;`.

6. **Out of Area (`src/components/ward-management/out-of-area/out-of-area-third-edition.module.css`):**
   - Changed `.boardGrid, .ledgerGrid` from `align-items: start` to `align-items: stretch`.
   - Clamped `.registerColumn .panel` and `.detailColumn .panel` on desktop to `height: clamp(32rem, calc(100dvh - 16rem), 54rem)`.
   - Pinned `.ph` headers and sticky table column headers (`.table thead th { position: sticky; top: 0; z-index: 2; }`), allowing long inpatients lists and repatriation case inspector details to scroll smoothly without leaving grey space.
   - Balanced `.secondaryGrid` (`.groupSummary` and `.provenancePanel`) with `align-items: stretch` and `height: 100%`.

7. **Audited Screens with Existing Verified Fixed Architecture:**
   - **Community** (`community.module.css`): Lines 1342–1426 implement Q003 matched panel heights (`align-items: stretch`, `clamp(22rem, calc(100dvh - 18rem), 34rem)` and `clamp(32rem, calc(100dvh - 10rem), 52rem)`), pinned headers, and scrolling bodies.

### Issues Added to Ledger

1. **WF-FIX-01 · P2 · High-DPI Zoom Scale & Virtual Keyboard Viewport Dynamic Resizing:**
   - _Observation:_ The `clamp(32rem, calc(100dvh - 16rem), 54rem)` formulas rely on dynamic viewport units (`100dvh`). On mobile devices when the software keyboard opens, some mobile browsers reduce `100dvh` or reposition sticky headers.
   - _Required action:_ Verify and enhance with `window.visualViewport` listeners or CSS `interactive-widget=resizes-content` viewport meta if mobile keyboard entry causes viewport compression on form fields within clamped workspaces.

2. **WF-FIX-02 · P3 · Keyboard Focus Ring Scrollport Auto-Centering Margin:**
   - _Observation:_ Inside fixed scrollable containers (`.panelBody`, `.attentionWrap`, `.lstWrap`), when a keyboard-only user tabs sequentially through dozens of items, the browser natively scrolls the focused element into view. In some desktop zoom configurations (>150%), the focused row's bottom border or ring can touch the scrollport container edge.
   - _Required action:_ Add `scroll-padding: 0.5rem` to internal scroll containers to provide a comfortable breathing margin around focused elements.

3. **WF-FIX-03 · P3 · Sticky Header Scroll-Activated Elevation Indicator:**
   - _Observation:_ Pinned panel headers (`.ph`, `.tabbar`, table `thead th`) remain fixed at the top while content scrolls beneath them. A subtle shadow or divider state that activates only while the scrollport is scrolled (`data-scrolled="true"`) could further clarify depth during mid-scroll interactions.
   - _Required action:_ Add a lightweight scroll-driven CSS animation (`animation-timeline: scroll()`) or attribute toggle to enhance elevation depth when scrolling starts.

   - Separated the scrubber cursor DOM structure into a distinct circular `.ganttCursorPin` and floating `.ganttCursorTime` badge, adding a left clamp when `scrub === 0` to eliminate visual collision with the 220px left axis.

## §7.70 · Full-Estate 41-Route and Interactive Component Audit Closeout

**Audit & Alignment Completed: 25 September 2026**

### Context & Mission

Perform an exhaustive, meticulous audit of all 41 routes and every interactive component in the Ward Flow bed-coordination application, fix any defects found, enforce D4/D5 clinical boundaries (no invented statutory countdowns or section numbers; unwired controls labeled "Not wired in this prototype"), eliminate dead ends, and verify complete functionality with automated tests.

1. **Universal Shell, Header & Navigation Rail:**
   - **Route Title & Place Resolution:** Enhanced `resolveWardScreenTitle` in `src/components/ward-management/ward-nav.ts` with explicit route prefix handlers for `/board`, `/ward`, `/ed`, `/community`, `/settings`, and `/sovereign`.
   - **Header Subtitle Resolution:** Fixed regex check in `ward-bar.tsx` replacing dead `units.some(...)` call with `/^\/mockups\/ward-flow\/board\//u.test(pathname)`.
   - **Modal Focus Management:** Attached explicit `returnFocusRef` to Activity, Tasks, and Tools `<Sheet>` triggers in `ward-bar.tsx`, verifying full `role="dialog"`, `aria-modal="true"`, focus trapping, and focus restoration upon Escape.
   - **Active Rail Highlighting:** Refactored `isActiveRailEntry` in `ward-rail.tsx` with path normalization and subpath alias matching across all routes.
   - **Pinned Contract Safeguards:** Verified `WARD_PRIMARY_ACTIONS` remains strictly pinned to 20 routes; no `/alerts` or `/on-call` added to `RAIL_GROUPS`.

2. **Core Operations & Capacity:**
   - **Event Propagation & Stop-Propagation:** Added `event.stopPropagation()` to candidate allocation buttons and refresh triggers in `capacity-screen.tsx` (`NetworkRow`), preventing row toggle collisions.
   - **Bed Map Clickability:** Verified all bed map cells and cards cleanly open unit details sidebars without unhandled bubbling.
   - **Prototype Footers:** Standardized single `<WardPrototypeFooter testId="ward-delays-governance" />` in `delays-screen.tsx`.

3. **Specialist & Departmental Boards:**
   - **In-Page Hash Tab Synchronization:** In `ward-screen.tsx`, added `useEffect` listening to `window.location.hash` and `hashchange` to automatically switch `activeTab` to `"return"` when targeting `#bed-capacity`, `#ward-daily-return`, `#ward-flag-bed-release`, or `#ward-leave-bed-form`. Added explicit `onClick={() => setActiveTab("return")}` to hero link `#bed-capacity`.
   - **Bed Release State Label Accessibility:** Restored `<strong>` wrapping on `bedReleaseStateLabels` for both expected and confirmed bed releases in `ward-screen.tsx` while keeping `.cardBedName` bold, aligning sentence-case display label assertions in `tests/ward-screen.dom.test.tsx`.
   - **ED Census Triage Form Expansion:** Added `receiptCorrectionOpen` to the row rendering condition enclosing `<tr className={styles.subFormRow}>` in `ed-screen.tsx` so Form 1A receipt corrections expand smoothly.
   - **Single Footer Standardization:** Replaced raw governance div in `officer-screen.tsx` with `<WardPrototypeFooter testId="ward-officer-governance" note="All outstanding jobs; closed movements excluded · Providers identify organisations · Not a medical device" />`.

4. **Referrals, Patient Caseload & Specialist Modules:**
   - **Dead-End Resolution:** Added missing `onClick` and `onKeyDown` keyboard activation handlers to SVG duration points and referral source proportional bars in `statistics-community-screen.tsx`, eliminating all dead controls.
   - **Strict D5 Statutory Compliance:** In `patient-search.tsx`, eliminated synthetic statutory deadline calculations (`statutoryHours = 72 : 168`) and removed defaulting queued referrals to `Form 1A` (now set to "Not recorded" and "No due time recorded").
   - **Search Hotkey Guard:** Added `tagName !== "select"` check to `/` keydown handler in `patient-search.tsx` so select dropdowns do not lose focus.
   - **Referral Drawer Copy & Modality:** Standardized copy in `ward-referral-drawer.tsx` from "Statutory Benchmark" to "Operational Acuity Target", added `role="dialog"` and `aria-modal="true"`.
   - **Footer Test IDs:** Standardized footers with explicit `testId` across `legal-forms-screen.tsx` (`ward-legal-forms-governance`), `alerts-screen.tsx` (`ward-alerts-governance`), and `sovereign-showcase-screen.tsx` (`ward-sovereign-governance`).
